Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the physical_chemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 198 and the convergence rate is O(333/n^1).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 198 + O(1/n^333). Summing over all modes and applying the physical_chemistry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
